P1203: پیوست‌ها
P1204: ================================================================================
P1205: پیوست الف: کدهای پیاده‌سازی و توابع کلیدی پایتون (Python Source Codes)
P1206: ================================================================================
P1207: 
P1208: این پیوست شامل توابع اصلی پایتون مربوط به فازهای چهارگانه سیستم هیبریدی پیشنهادی می‌باشد:
P1209: 
P1210: ۱. تابع قطعه‌بندی U-Net و استخراج کلید پویا SHA-256:
P1211: --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
P1212: import torch
P1213: import hashlib
P1214: import numpy as np
P1215: 
P1216: def extract_roi_and_generate_key(unet_model, medical_image_tensor):
P1217:     """
P1218:     ورودی: تصویر پزشکی به صورت تینسر Torch
P1219:     خروجی: ماسک قطعه‌بندی ROI و کلید هَش ۲۵۶ بیتی K_hash
P1220:     """
P1221:     unet_model.eval()
P1222:     with torch.no_grad():
P1223:         roi_mask = unet_model(medical_image_tensor)
P1224:         roi_mask = (roi_mask > 0.5).float()
P1225:     
P1226:     # استخراج گشتاورهای آماری ناحیه ROI
P1227:     roi_pixels = medical_image_tensor * roi_mask
P1228:     mean_val = torch.mean(roi_pixels).item()
P1229:     std_val = torch.std(roi_pixels).item()
P1230:     var_val = torch.var(roi_pixels).item()
P1231:     
P1232:     # ترکیب گشتاورها و تولید هَش SHA-256
P1233:     stat_str = f"{mean_val:.8f}_{std_val:.8f}_{var_val:.8f}"
P1234:     k_hash_hex = hashlib.sha256(stat_str.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()
P1235:     k_hash_bits = bin(int(k_hash_hex, 16))[2:].zfill(256)
P1236:     
P1237:     return roi_mask, k_hash_hex, k_hash_bits
P1238: 
P1239: 
P1240: ۲. تابع سیستم ۵بعدی ابرآشوبی (Runge-Kutta 4th Order):
P1241: --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
P1242: def hyperchaos_5d_system(state, a=10.0, b=8/3, c=28.0, d=3.0, e=0.2):
P1243:     x, y, z, u, v = state
P1244:     dx = a * (y - x) + u
P1245:     dy = c * x - x * z + d * y + v
P1246:     dz = x * y - b * z
P1247:     du = -y * z + e * u
P1248:     dv = x * z - e * v
P1249:     return np.array([dx, dy, dz, du, dv])
P1250: 
P1251: def generate_5d_chaotic_sequences(k_hash_hex, num_samples):
P1252:     # تبدیل کلید هَش ۲۵۶ بیتی به شرایط اولیه سیستم
P1253:     init_x = int(k_hash_hex[0:12], 16) / (16**12)
P1254:     init_y = int(k_hash_hex[12:24], 16) / (16**12)
P1255:     init_z = int(k_hash_hex[24:36], 16) / (16**12)
P1256:     init_u = int(k_hash_hex[36:48], 16) / (16**12)
P1257:     init_v = int(k_hash_hex[48:60], 16) / (16**12)
P1258:     
P1259:     state = np.array([init_x, init_y, init_z, init_u, init_v])
P1260:     dt = 0.001
P1261:     seqs = np.zeros((num_samples, 5))
P1262:     
P1263:     # حل عددی به روش Runge-Kutta مرتبه ۴
P1264:     for i in range(num_samples):
P1265:         k1 = hyperchaos_5d_system(state)
P1266:         k2 = hyperchaos_5d_system(state + 0.5 * dt * k1)
P1267:         k3 = hyperchaos_5d_system(state + 0.5 * dt * k2)
P1268:         k4 = hyperchaos_5d_system(state + dt * k3)
P1269:         state = state + (dt / 6.0) * (k1 + 2*k2 + 2*k3 + k4)
P1270:         seqs[i] = state
P1271:         
P1272:     return seqs
P1273: 
P1274: 
P1275: ۳. تابع جایگشت زیگ‌زاگی و انتشار پویای DNA:
P1276: --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
P1277: def zigzag_permutation(image_matrix):
P1278:     h, w = image_matrix.shape
P1279:     result = np.zeros_like(image_matrix)
P1280:     # الگوریتم جایگشت متقاطع زیگ‌زاگی
P1281:     indices = []
P1282:     for i in range(h + w - 1):
P1283:         if i % 2 == 0:
P1284:             x = i if i < h else h - 1
P1285:             y = 0 if i < h else i - h + 1
P1286:             while x >= 0 and y < w:
P1287:                 indices.append((x, y))
P1288:                 x -= 1
P1289:                 y += 1
P1290:         else:
P1291:             x = 0 if i < w else i - w + 1
P1292:             y = i if i < w else w - 1
P1293:             while x < h and y >= 0:
P1294:                 indices.append((x, y))
P1295:                 x += 1
P1296:                 y -= 1
P1297:     flat_img = image_matrix.flatten()
P1298:     scrambled = np.zeros_like(flat_img)
P1299:     for new_idx, (r, c) in enumerate(indices):
P1300:         scrambled[new_idx] = image_matrix[r, c]
P1301:     return scrambled.reshape(h, w)
P1302: 
P1303: 
P1304: ۴. تابع پنهان‌نگاری Saliency-aware و فشرده‌سازی zlib:
P1305: --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
P1306: import zlib
P1307: 
P1308: def embed_metadata_saliency(image_matrix, metadata_bytes, non_roi_mask):
P1309:     # فشرده‌سازی zlib فراداده
P1310:     compressed_meta = zlib.compress(metadata_bytes, level=9)
P1311:     meta_bits = ''.join(format(byte, '08b') for byte in compressed_meta)
P1312:     
P1313:     stego_img = image_matrix.copy().flatten()
P1314:     mask_flat = non_roi_mask.flatten()
P1315:     
P1316:     bit_idx = 0
P1317:     for pixel_idx in range(len(stego_img)):
P1318:         if mask_flat[pixel_idx] == 1 and bit_idx < len(meta_bits):
P1319:             # جای‌دهی ۲ بیت کم‌ارزش LSB
P1320:             val = stego_img[pixel_idx]
P1321:             val = (val & 0xFC) | int(meta_bits[bit_idx:bit_idx+2], 2)
P1322:             stego_img[pixel_idx] = val
P1323:             bit_idx += 2
P1324:             
P1325:     return stego_img.reshape(image_matrix.shape)

P1356: پیوست ج: مشخصات مجموعه‌دادگان مرجع و بستر سخت‌افزاری NVIDIA Jetson TX2
P1357: ================================================================================
P1358: 
P1359: ۱. مشخصات مجموعه‌دادگان مرجع:
P1360: - دادگان DRIVE و RITE: تصاویر فاندوس شبکیه چشم با ابعاد 565x584 پیکسل جهت قطعه‌بندی عروق و ROI شبکیه.
P1361: - دادگان BraTS 2020: اسکن‌های 3D MRI مغزی در مدالیته‌های T1, T2, T1ce, FLAIR با ابعاد 240x240 پیکسل.
P1362: - دادگان COVID-19 CXR: تصاویر رادیوگرافی قفسه سینه و CT ریه با ابعاد 1024x1024 پیکسل جهت تشخیص عفونت‌های ریوی.
P1363: 
P1364: ۲. مشخصات بستر سخت‌افزاری NVIDIA Jetson TX2:
P1365: - پردازنده اصلی (CPU): Dual-Core NVIDIA Denver 2 64-Bit + Quad-Core ARM Cortex-A57
P1366: - پردازنده گرافیکی (GPU): 256-core NVIDIA Pascal Architecture (1.3 TFLOPS)
P1367: - حافظه اصلی (RAM): 8 GB 128-bit LPDDR4 (59.7 GB/s)
P1368: - توان مصرفی (Power Consumption): 7.5W / 15W (کم‌مصرف پردازش لبه IoMT)
P1369: - سیستم‌عامل و بستر نرم‌افزاری: Ubuntu 18.04 LTS (JetPack SDK 4.4), PyTorch 1.8, OpenCV 4.5, CUDA 10.2
P1370: Abstract
P1371: With the expansion of e-health systems, telemedicine, and the Internet of Medical Things, the secure transmission of medical images over open networks has become a fundamental challenge in health information security. Owing to their high volume, extensive redundancy, and strong correlation between adjacent pixels, medical images call for protection mechanisms that go beyond classical text-oriented encryption; patient metadata is also in need of independent protection. This thesis designs an integrated framework for enhancing the security and privacy of medical images that combines three components in a single processing chain: deep U-Net segmentation for identifying sensitive regions and extracting content-dependent statistical features, a five-dimensional hyper-chaotic system for generating pseudo-random sequences used in the confusion and diffusion phases, and saliency-based steganography for imperceptibly embedding compressed patient metadata into visually unattended regions. The present version focuses on design and the elaboration of the evaluation plan: experiments are defined on public datasets of retinal images (DRIVE and RITE), brain MRI (BraTS), and chest X-rays, with Shannon entropy, adjacent-pixel correlation, NPCR, UACI, PSNR, SSIM, and execution time as evaluation criteria, and with comparative scenarios against selected methods from the literature. Based on theoretical analyses and foundational studies, the encryption core is expected to reach an entropy close to the ideal value of 8 bits, differential measures within standard bounds (NPCR around 99.6 percent and UACI around 33.3 percent), and the steganography module a PSNR above 40 dB; final confirmation of these values depends on the practical implementation and analysis of the planned experiments. By integrating deep learning, chaos theory, and adaptive steganography, the proposed framework takes a step toward privacy-aware, secure e-health and Internet of Medical Things systems.
P1372: Keywords: Medical Image Security, Hyper-chaotic Encryption, Deep Learning, U-Net, Steganography, Privacy, Internet of Medical Things.
P1373: 
P1374:
